    package '02_Functional' {
        package '00_UseCaseDefinitions' {
            use case def ReviewAndApproveStepResult;
            use case def AssessLegacyData;
            use case def PrepareDataForSynthesis;
            use case def ApplySemanticGuardrails;
            use case def ResolveAmbiguity;

        package '01_CoreUseCases' {
            use case <UC_000> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::ReviewAndApproveStepResult {
                doc
                /* Goal
                 * Ensure human supervision of automated intermediate processing results.
                 *
                 * Preconditions
                 * A processing step has generated one or more candidate results
                 * requiring human review.
                 *
                 * Main Success Scenario
                 * 1. The system presents the candidate result and its applicable
                 *    explanation and traceability information.
                 * 2. The user reviews the presented result.
                 * 3. The user modifies or selects the preferred result where applicable.
                 * 4. The system records the explicit human review decision.
                 * 5. An approved intermediate result may be used by the explicitly
                 *    defined subsequent processing step.
                 *
                 * Postconditions
                 * A human-reviewed intermediate result and its review decision are
                 * available.
                 *
                 * This use case establishes authority only for the reviewed intermediate
                 * result within its defined processing context. It does not establish
                 * Final Model Authority.
                 */
                dependency from UC_000 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Expert Review of Generated Interpretations';
                dependency from UC_000 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Human Intervention for Conflicts';
                dependency from UC_000 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Explanation of Generated Engineering Results';
                dependency from UC_000 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Model Content Validation';
                subject ;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_01;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_02;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_03;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_04;
                objective {
                    doc
                    /* Review, adjust and explicitly authorize an intermediate result
                     * for its defined subsequent processing context.
                     */
                }
                item 'input: CandidateStepResult';
                item 'output: ReviewedIntermediateResult';
            }
            use case <UC_001> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::AssessLegacyData {
                doc
                /* Goal
                 * Assess the quality and suitability of legacy engineering data.
                 *
                 * Preconditions
                 * Legacy engineering data is available.
                 *
                 * Main Success Scenario
                 * 1. The user selects legacy engineering data.
                 * 2. The system evaluates completeness.
                 * 3. The system evaluates consistency.
                 * 4. The system evaluates reliability and relevance.
                 * 5. The system generates an assessment report.
                 * 6. The user reviews and approves the assessment report.
                 *
                 * Postconditions
                 * Validated assessment information is available for subsequent processing.*/
                include use case reviewAndApprove ::> UC_000;
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Input Data Suitability Assessment';
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Input Data Completeness Check';
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Input Data Consistency Check';
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Source Reliability Classification';
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Source Relevance Classification';
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Input Constraint Violation Detection';
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Legacy Data Completion';
                subject ;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_01;
                objective {
                    doc /* Assess the suitability of legacy engineering data for automated processing.*/
                }
                doc /**/
                item 'input: LegacyEngineeringData' {
                    doc /**/
                }
                item 'output: DataAssessmentReport';
            }
            use case <UC_002> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::PrepareDataForSynthesis {

                /* Goal
                 * Ensure that legacy engineering data is complete and suitable for synthesis.

                 * Preconditions

            use case <UC_005> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::SynthesizeSysMLv2Model {
                doc
                /* Goal
                 * Synthesize a traceable SysML v2 model representation from engineering
                 * information admitted to the current project.
                 *
                 * Preconditions
                 * Source-local engineering information required for synthesis has
                 * completed its applicable Human Review.
                 * The applicable source contributions have passed the Project Fit gate.
                 *
                 * Main Success Scenario
                 * 1. The user initiates model synthesis.
                 * 2. The system derives one Model Candidate Set from the admitted
                 *    engineering information.
                 * 3. The system preserves the contribution and traceability of each
                 *    originating engineering source.
                 * 4. The system structures the generated candidate content according to
                 *    the applicable target-model structure.
                 * 5. The Model Candidate Set is presented for the required intermediate
                 *    Human Review.
                 * 6. The user reviews and approves the applicable candidate content for
                 *    subsequent model synthesis.
                 * 7. The system assembles the human-authorized candidate content into the
                 *    resulting model representation.
                 * 8. The system generates the corresponding SysML v2 textual artifact set.
                 *
                 * Postconditions
                 * A Generated SysML Artifact Set is available for automated validation.
                 * No Final Model Authority or publication authority has been established.
                 */
                include use case reviewAndApprove ::> UC_000;
                dependency from UC_005 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Architecture Information Derivation';
                dependency from UC_005 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'02_Architectural'::'Structural Pattern Conformance';
                dependency from UC_005 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'02_Architectural'::'Model Reusability';
                dependency from UC_005 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'02_Architectural'::'Modular Model Growth';
                dependency from UC_005 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'02_Architectural'::'Non Destructive Submodel Integration';
                dependency from UC_005 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Source Traceability';
                dependency from UC_005 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'02_Architectural'::'Reusable Architectural Pattern Application';
                dependency from UC_005 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Multiple Source Support';
                dependency from UC_005 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Architecture as Code Provision';
                dependency from UC_005 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'SysML v2 Target Representation';
                subject ;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_01;
                objective {
                    doc
                    /* Generate a traceable SysML v2 artifact set from Project-Fit-admitted
                     * engineering information and human-authorized model candidates.
                     */
                }
                item 'input: AdmittedEngineeringInformation';
                item 'output: GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet';
            }
            use case <UC_006> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::ValidateGeneratedArchitecture {
                doc
                /* Goal
                 * Validate the generated SysML v2 artifact set and provide explicit
                 * validation evidence for Final Model Review.
                 *
                 * Preconditions
                 * A Generated SysML Artifact Set is available.
                 *
                 * Main Success Scenario
                 * 1. The system validates the integrity and consistency of the generated
                 *    artifact set.
                 * 2. The system validates conformance to the applicable SysML v2 textual
                 *    notation rules.
                 * 3. The system validates the applicable artifact-structure and
                 *    generation-policy constraints.
                 * 4. The system validates relationship, endpoint and traceability
                 *    consistency within the generated artifact set.
                 * 5. The system records all validation findings in an explicit validation
                 *    result.
                 *
                 * Postconditions
                 * A SysML Validation Result is available for Final Model Review.
                 * Validation does not modify generated model content.
                 * Validation does not establish Final Model Authority.
                 */
                dependency from UC_006 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Model Content Validation';
                dependency from UC_006 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'SysML v2 Target Representation';
                dependency from UC_006 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Architecture as Code Provision';
                dependency from UC_006 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Source Traceability';
                dependency from UC_006 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Explanation of Generated Engineering Results';
                subject ;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_03;
                objective {
                    doc
                    /* Validate the generated SysML v2 artifact set and provide explicit
                     * validation evidence without modifying the generated model or
                     * establishing model authority.
                     */
                }
                item 'input: GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet';
                item 'output: SysMLValidationResult';
            }
            use case <UC_007> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::AssessProjectSourceAdmissibility {
                doc
                /* Goal
                 * Determine whether the current reviewed engineering source
                 * contributions are admissible for use within the active engineering
                 * project.
                 *
                 * Preconditions
                 * Current source-local reviewed engineering information is available
                 * for the applicable engineering sources.
                 *
                 * Main Success Scenario
                 * 1. The system identifies the current reviewed engineering sources.
                 * 2. The system assesses the applicability of each source contribution
                 *    to the active engineering project.
                 * 3. The system preserves the exact project, source and processing
                 *    context of each assessment.
                 * 4. The system identifies source contributions requiring explicit
                 *    Human resolution.
                 * 5. The Information Manager resolves such cases where required.
                 * 6. The system records the resulting Project Fit state.
                 *
                 * Postconditions
                 * The admissibility state of each applicable current engineering source
                 * contribution is known.
                 * Only admitted contributions may proceed to project-level model
                 * synthesis.
                 *
                 * Automatic cross-source semantic reconciliation is not part of this
                 * use case.
                 */
                dependency from UC_007 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Multiple Source Support';
                dependency from UC_007 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Source Traceability';
                dependency from UC_007 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Source Relevance Classification';
                dependency from UC_007 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'02_Architectural'::'Project Information Isolation';
                dependency from UC_007 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Human Review Decision Association';
                dependency from UC_007 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Approved Information Boundary';
                subject ;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_04;
                objective {
                    doc
                    /* Establish the project admissibility of current reviewed source
                     * contributions before model synthesis.
                     */
                }
                item 'input: SourceLocalReviewedEngineeringInformation';
                item 'output: ProjectFitState';
            }
            use case <UC_008> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::ReviewAndAuthorizeFinalModel {
                doc
                /* Goal
                 * Perform the final Human review of the complete generated SysML v2 model
                 * representation and explicitly establish Final Model Authority.
                 *
                 * Preconditions
                 * A Generated SysML Artifact Set and its corresponding SysML Validation
                 * Result are available.
                 *
                 * Main Success Scenario
                 * 1. The system presents the Generated SysML Artifact Set.
                 * 2. The system presents the corresponding validation findings,
                 *    explanation and traceability information.
                 * 3. The System Architect and System Modeler review the complete generated
                 *    model representation.
                 * 4. The reviewers assess identified validation findings and the
                 *    engineering content represented by the generated model.
                 * 5. The reviewers reject the result or request correction where the
                 *    generated model is not acceptable.
                 * 6. The reviewers record an explicit Final Model Review decision.
                 * 7. If the exact reviewed artifact set is approved and all mandatory
                 *    publication conditions are satisfied, Final Model Authority is
                 *    established for that reviewed model revision.
                 *
                 * Postconditions
                 * An explicit Final Model Review decision exists for the exact reviewed
                 * Generated SysML Artifact Set.
                 * An approved decision establishes Final Model Authority only for that
                 * exact reviewed model revision.
                 * Final publication remains prohibited unless the applicable validation
                 * and release conditions are satisfied.
                 */
                dependency from UC_008 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Model Content Validation';
                dependency from UC_008 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Human Review Decision Association';
                dependency from UC_008 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Approved Information Boundary';
                dependency from UC_008 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Explanation of Generated Engineering Results';
                dependency from UC_008 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Source Traceability';
                subject ;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_03;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_01;
                objective {
                    doc
                    /* Review and explicitly authorize the exact generated SysML v2 model
                     * revision before final engineering publication.
                     */
                }
                item 'input: GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet';
                item 'input: SysMLValidationResult';
                item 'output: FinalModelReviewDecision';
            }
        }
        package '02_Views' {
            view GV_UseCaseOverview : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::gv {
                doc
                /* Overview of the Stakeholder-level functional use cases and
                 * participating human roles of the Turing Generator.
                 *
                 * The view represents stakeholder interaction and functional intent.
                 * It does not define System Functions, Logical Components or
                 * subsystem boundaries.
                 */
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_000;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_001;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_002;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_003;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_004;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_005;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_006;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_007;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_008;
                expose '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_01;
                expose '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_02;
                expose '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_03;
                expose '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_04;
            }
        }
        package '03_Traceability' {
            view UC_SR_Traceability : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::gv {
                doc
                /* Traceability view between Stakeholder-level use cases and
                 * Stakeholder Requirements.
                 *
                 * Use Case dependencies represent the Stakeholder Requirements
                 * addressed by each interaction scenario.
                 *
                 * The traceability represented here is stakeholder-level functional
                 * traceability and shall not be interpreted as System Function or
                 * subsystem allocation.
                 */
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_000;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_001;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_002;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_003;

                expose '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::*;
            }
        }
    }
    package '03_Logical' {
        doc
        /* Stakeholder Logical modeling note:
         * This package defines the external logical context of the Turing Generator
         * as the System of Interest.
         * It represents the human stakeholder roles, external engineering source
         * information and stakeholder-visible information exchanges across the
         * system boundary.
         * It does not define internal System Functions, Logical Components,
         * subsystems, software modules, technologies or deployment structures.
         */
        package '00_ContextDefinitions' {
            part def ContextElement;
            port def ContextInteractionPort;
            interface def ContextInteraction {
                end sourceEnd : ContextInteractionPort;
                end targetEnd : ContextInteractionPort;
            }
            part def StakeholderContextActor :> ContextElement {
                doc
                /* Logical occurrence of an existing human stakeholder role
                 * within the Turing Generator system context.
                 */
                port systemPort : ContextInteractionPort;
            }
            part def 'Engineering Source' :> ContextElement {
                doc
                /* External engineering source artifact or source-information
                 * context registered for processing by the Turing Generator.
                 */
                port systemPort : ContextInteractionPort;
            }
            part def 'Turing Generator System' :> ContextElement {
                doc
                /* System of Interest that transforms heterogeneous legacy
                 * engineering information into traceable SysML v2 architecture
                 * artifacts under explicit human review and authority.
                 */
                port sourceInformationPort : ContextInteractionPort;
                port modelerInteractionPort : ContextInteractionPort;
                port domainInteractionPort : ContextInteractionPort;
                port architectureInteractionPort : ContextInteractionPort;
                port informationGovernancePort : ContextInteractionPort;
            }
        }
        package '01_SystemContext' {
            part <CTX_001> 'Turing Generator Stakeholder Context' {
                doc
                /* External logical context of the Turing Generator.
                 * The Turing Generator is the System of Interest.
                 * Human stakeholder roles and Engineering Sources remain outside
                 * the system boundary.
                 * The represented interactions describe stakeholder-visible
                 * information exchange and authority interaction only.
                 */
                part turingGenerator : '00_ContextDefinitions'::'Turing Generator System';
                part engineeringSources : '00_ContextDefinitions'::'Engineering Source';
                part systemModeler : '00_ContextDefinitions'::StakeholderContextActor;
                part domainExpert : '00_ContextDefinitions'::StakeholderContextActor;
                part systemArchitect : '00_ContextDefinitions'::StakeholderContextActor;
                part informationManager : '00_ContextDefinitions'::StakeholderContextActor;
                dependency from systemModeler to '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_01;
                dependency from domainExpert to '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_02;
                dependency from systemArchitect to '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_03;
                dependency from informationManager to '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_04;
                interface engineeringSourceExchange : '00_ContextDefinitions'::ContextInteraction
                        connect sourceEnd ::> engineeringSources.systemPort
                        to targetEnd ::> turingGenerator.sourceInformationPort;

                interface modelerInteraction : '00_ContextDefinitions'::ContextInteraction
                        connect sourceEnd ::> systemModeler.systemPort
                        to targetEnd ::> turingGenerator.modelerInteractionPort;

                interface domainClarificationInteraction : '00_ContextDefinitions'::ContextInteraction
                        connect sourceEnd ::> domainExpert.systemPort
                        to targetEnd ::> turingGenerator.domainInteractionPort;

                interface architectureReviewAndAuthorityInteraction : '00_ContextDefinitions'::ContextInteraction
                        connect sourceEnd ::> systemArchitect.systemPort to targetEnd ::> turingGenerator.architectureInteractionPort;

                interface informationGovernanceInteraction : '00_ContextDefinitions'::ContextInteraction
                        connect sourceEnd ::> informationManager.systemPort
                        to targetEnd ::> turingGenerator.informationGovernancePort;
            }
        }
        package '02_Views' {
            view IV_TuringGeneratorStakeholderContext : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::iv {
                doc
                /* Stakeholder context diagram of the Turing Generator.
                 * The view exposes the System of Interest together with the
                 * human stakeholder roles and external Engineering Sources.
                 * Context interactions represent information and authority
                 * exchange across the system boundary.
                 * Internal System Functions, Logical Components and subsystem
                 * structures are intentionally not shown.
                 */
                expose '01_SystemContext'::CTX_001::turingGenerator;
                expose '01_SystemContext'::CTX_001::engineeringSources;
                expose '01_SystemContext'::CTX_001::systemModeler;
                expose '01_SystemContext'::CTX_001::domainExpert;
                expose '01_SystemContext'::CTX_001::systemArchitect;
                expose '01_SystemContext'::CTX_001::informationManager;
            }
        }
    }

package '02_System' {
    package '01_Requirements' {
        doc
        /* System Requirement modeling note:
         *
         * The System Requirements were derived individually from the accepted Stakeholder Requirements.
         * The topical package grouping was applied retrospectively for navigation and documentation only.
         * It shall not be interpreted as a pre-allocation to Logical Components or as a definition of subsystem boundaries.
         * System Functions and Logical Components are derived only after this System Requirement baseline is accepted.
         */
        package '00_RequirementDefinitions' {
            requirement def BaseSystemRequirement {
                attribute rationale;
                attribute source;
                attribute scope;
            }
            requirement def SystemRequirement :> BaseSystemRequirement;
            requirement def DataSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
            requirement def FunctionalSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
            requirement def AssuranceSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
            requirement def SemanticSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
            requirement def ArchitectureSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
            requirement def ValidationSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
            requirement def UserInterfaceSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
        }
        package '01_InputSuitabilityAndQuality' {
            requirement <SYSR_001> 'Assess Source Processing Suitability' : '00_RequirementDefinitions'::DataSystemRequirement {
                doc /* The system shall determine whether each registered engineering source is suitable for the selected automated processing task based on the applicable processing and information-quality criteria. */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /* Establishes a task-specific suitability decision that goes beyond technical file readability. */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /* SR_001 Input Data Suitability Assessment */
                }
                attribute :>> scope {
                    doc /* mvp */
                }
                dependency
                        from 'Assess Source Processing Suitability'
                        to '01_Stakeholder'::'01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Input Data Suitability Assessment';
            }
            requirement <SYSR_002> 'Extract Processable Source Information' : '00_RequirementDefinitions'::FunctionalSystemRequirement {
                doc
                /* The system shall create a deterministic, persistable and
                 * source-traceable processable projection of each supported registered
                 * engineering source before semantic processing.
                 */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc
                    /* Establishes a reproducible machine-processable representation while
                     * preserving the relationship to the registered source and separating
                     * deterministic source preparation from semantic interpretation.
                     */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc
                    /* SR_001 Input Data Suitability Assessment;
                     * SR_019 Source Traceability;
                     *
                     * Changed due to ADR-011 Semantic Information Unit and Ontology Boundary
                     */

                    doc /* mvp */
                }

                /* The system shall identify contradictory or mutually inconsistent
                 * information within the source-local engineering information assessed
                 * for the selected processing task.
                 */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc
                    /* Performs consistency assessment within one source-processing context

                doc /* The system shall integrate a generated model unit through defined integration relationships without restructuring existing model elements. */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /* Requires integration through explicit relationships rather than silent restructuring of existing model content. */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /* SR_010 Non Destructive Submodel Integration */

        package '10_DesignConstraints' {
            doc
            /* System Design Constraint modeling note:
             *
             * The constraints in this package document intentionally selected solution-space boundary conditions.
             * Each constraint contains one independently verifiable design obligation.
             * The category packages support navigation only and do not define Logical Components or subsystem boundaries.
             */
            package '00_ConstraintDefinitions' {
                requirement def BaseSystemDesignConstraint {
                    attribute rationale;
                    attribute source;
                    attribute scope;
                    attribute verificationQuestion;
                }
                requirement def SystemDesignConstraint :> BaseSystemDesignConstraint;
                requirement def ArchitectureConstraint :> SystemDesignConstraint {
                    doc /* Constraint related to the structural and orchestration architecture of the Turing Generator. */
                }
                requirement def AgentConstraint :> SystemDesignConstraint {
                    doc /* Constraint related to agent-based candidate generation and agent selection. */
                }
                requirement def RepresentationConstraint :> SystemDesignConstraint {
                    doc /* Constraint related to target notation, artifact structure and validation compatibility. */
                }
                requirement def DataConstraint :> SystemDesignConstraint {
                    doc /* Constraint related to technical identities, operational records and artifact integrity. */
                }
                requirement def UserInterfaceConstraint :> SystemDesignConstraint {
                    doc /* Constraint related to the user interface and its access to system capabilities. */
                }
            }
            package '01_ArchitectureAndOrchestration' {
                requirement <SYSC_001> 'Bounded Processing Responsibility' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::ArchitectureConstraint {
                    doc /* The system architecture shall assign each processing capability one bounded primary responsibility. */
                    attribute :>> rationale {
                        doc /* Ensures that processing capabilities remain responsibility-focused and independently understandable. */
                    }
                    attribute :>> source {
                        doc /* Former SYSC_001 ModularPipelineArchitecture; Turing Generator project scope */
                    }
                    attribute :>> scope {
                        doc /* mvp */
                    }
                    attribute :>> verificationQuestion {
                        doc /* Does each processing capability have exactly one clearly bounded primary responsibility? */
                    }
                    dependency
                            from 'Bounded Processing Responsibility'
                            to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'02_ArchitectureDerivationAndStructure'::'Derive Architecture Information';
                }
                requirement <SYSC_022> 'Explicit Artifact Exchange' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::ArchitectureConstraint {
                    doc /* Processing capabilities shall exchange processing information through explicitly represented artifacts. */
                    attribute :>> rationale {
                        doc /* Prevents hidden cross-capability state exchange and preserves traceable processing boundaries. */
                    }
                    attribute :>> source {
                        doc /* Turing Generator artifact-driven architecture; former SYSC_001 ModularPipelineArchitecture */
                    }
                    attribute :>> scope {
                        doc /* mvp */

                }
                requirement <SYSC_003> 'Defined Capability Contract' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::ArchitectureConstraint {
                    doc /* Each processing capability shall expose a defined contract describing its accepted inputs, produced outputs, execution status, error reporting and artifact references. */
                    attribute :>> rationale {

                        doc /* mvp */

                    }

                            from 'Defined Capability Contract'
                            to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'04_SemanticsAndTraceability'::'Bind Source-Derived Information to Its Origin';

                    dependency
                            from 'Defined Capability Contract'
                            to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'09_GovernanceSourcesAndHistory'::'Bind Processing Run Context';

                    dependency
                            from 'Defined Capability Contract'
                            to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'09_GovernanceSourcesAndHistory'::'Associate Artifacts with Originating Processing Run';
                }
                requirement <SYSC_002> 'Dedicated Processing Orchestrator' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::ArchitectureConstraint {

                requirement <SYSC_024> 'Orchestrator-Controlled Run State Transitions' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::ArchitectureConstraint {
                    doc
                    /* The processing orchestrator shall be the sole processing capability
                     * authorized to record authoritative processing-run state transitions.
                     */
                    attribute :>> rationale {
                        doc
                        /* Preserves one controlled authority boundary for processing-run

                         * deterministically from the retained event history.
                         */
                    }

                        doc

                         */
                    }

                    }

                            to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Determine Current Source Processing State';

                        doc /* mvp */

    package '02_Functional' {
        doc
        /* System Functional modeling note:
         *
         * The System Functions describe the complete intended behavior of the
         * Turing Generator at system level. They cover MVP, target and
         * optional-future System Requirements and are independent of the
         * current implementation status.
         *
         * Each System Requirement has exactly one primary functional allocation.
         * This primary allocation supports readable traceability and later
         * implementation-status assessment. It does not prohibit supporting
         * interactions between System Functions.
         *
         * The System Functions were derived after acceptance of the complete
         * System Requirement and Design Constraint baseline. They shall not be
         * interpreted as Logical Components or subsystem boundaries.
         */
        package '00_FunctionDefinitions' {

                    /* Non-sequential interaction model for the System Functions.
                     *
                     * The flows describe authoritative engineering-information progression,
                     * information exchange, Human-review boundaries, processing evidence
                     * and workflow-state projection.
                     *
                     * Source-local processing and Human Engineering Review occur before
                     * Project Fit. Only Project-admitted source-local engineering authority
                     * inputs may contribute to the unified Model Candidate Set.
                     *
                     * The flows shall not be interpreted as one mandatory temporal
                     * execution sequence.
                     */
                    action manageContext : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' {
                        out item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                    }
                    action orchestrateRuns : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {
                        in item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                        out item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        out item processingAttemptContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;
                        out item processingState : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingState;
                    }
                    action assessAndPrepare : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {
                        in item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item processingAttemptContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;
                        out item sourceProjection : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;
                        out item preparedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PreparedEngineeringInformation;
                        out item assessmentEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::DataAssessmentEvidence;
                    }
                    action applySemantics : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Apply Semantic Governance' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item preparedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PreparedEngineeringInformation;
                        out item canonicalSubjects : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;
                        out item governedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation;
                        out item semanticEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticAssessmentEvidence;
                    }
                    action generateInterpretationCandidates : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item canonicalSubjects : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;
                        in item governedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation;
                        out item candidateSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringCandidateSet;
                        out item candidateEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CandidateEvidence;
                    }
                    action reviewSourceEngineering : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item assessmentEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::DataAssessmentEvidence;
                        in item semanticEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticAssessmentEvidence;
                        in item candidateSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringCandidateSet;
                        in item candidateEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CandidateEvidence;
                        in item processingEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;
                        out item reviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        out item approvedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;
                    }
                    action assessProjectFit : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {
                        in item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item processingAttemptContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;
                        in item sourceProjection : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;
                        in item approvedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;
                        out item projectFitAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectFitAssessment;
                    }
                    action admitProjectContributions : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {
                        in item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                        in item projectFitAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectFitAssessment;
                        in item approvedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;
                        out item admittedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                    }
                    action assessCoverage : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item admittedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                        in item processingEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;
                        out item coverageAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;
                    }
                    action selectDerivationStrategy : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item admittedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                        out item derivationContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelDerivationContext;
                    }
                    action deriveModelCandidates : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item admittedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                        in item coverageAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;
                        in item derivationContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelDerivationContext;
                        out item modelCandidateSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;
                    }
                    action reviewModelCandidates : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item modelCandidateSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;
                        in item processingEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;
                        out item reviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                    }
                    action deriveModelPlacement : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item modelCandidateSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;
                        in item candidateReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        out item placementProposals : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelPlacementProposalSet;
                    }
                    action reviewModelPlacement : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item placementProposals : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelPlacementProposalSet;
                        in item processingEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;
                        out item placementReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        out item approvedPlacementSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedModelPlacementSet;
                    }
                    action assembleInternalModel : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item admittedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                        in item modelCandidateSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;
                        in item approvedPlacementSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedModelPlacementSet;
                        out item internalEngineeringModel : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel;
                    }
                    action validateArchitecture : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate and Integrate Architecture Content' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item internalEngineeringModel : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel;
                        out item architectureFindings : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ArchitectureValidationFindingSet;
                        out item validatedInternalModel : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ValidatedInternalEngineeringModel;
                    }
                    action generateSysML : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate SysML v2 Architecture Artifacts' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item validatedInternalModel : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ValidatedInternalEngineeringModel;
                        out item generatedArtifactSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                    }
                    action validateSysML : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item generatedArtifactSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                        out item validationResult : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;
                    }
                    action finalModelReview : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item generatedArtifactSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                        in item validationResult : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;
                        in item processingEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;
                        out item finalReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                    }
                    action publicationGate : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {
                        in item generatedArtifactSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                        in item validationResult : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;
                        in item finalReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        out item publicationAuthorization : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization;
                    }
                    action maintainEvidence : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability' {
                        in item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item processingAttemptContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;
                        in item sourceProjection : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;
                        in item assessmentEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::DataAssessmentEvidence;
                        in item semanticEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticAssessmentEvidence;
                        in item candidateEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CandidateEvidence;
                        in item approvedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;
                        in item projectFitAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectFitAssessment;
                        in item coverageAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;
                        in item modelCandidateSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;
                        in item approvedPlacementSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedModelPlacementSet;
                        in item internalEngineeringModel : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel;
                        in item architectureFindings : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ArchitectureValidationFindingSet;
                        in item generatedArtifactSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                        in item validationResult : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;
                        in item sourceReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        in item modelCandidateReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        in item placementReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        in item finalReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        in item publicationAuthorization : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization;
                        out item processingEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;
                        out item processingHistory : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingHistory;
                    }
                    action presentStatus : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Present Processing Status and History' {
                        in item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                        in item processingState : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingState;
                        in item coverageAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;
                        in item processingEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;
                        in item processingHistory : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingHistory;
                        in item sourceReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        in item modelCandidateReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        in item placementReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        in item finalReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        in item validationResult : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;
                        in item publicationAuthorization : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization;
                        out item statusPresentation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::StatusPresentation;
                    }
                    flow from manageContext.projectAndSourceContext to orchestrateRuns.projectAndSourceContext;
                    flow from manageContext.projectAndSourceContext to assessAndPrepare.projectAndSourceContext;
                    flow from manageContext.projectAndSourceContext to assessProjectFit.projectAndSourceContext;
                    flow from manageContext.projectAndSourceContext to admitProjectContributions.projectAndSourceContext;
                    flow from manageContext.projectAndSourceContext to maintainEvidence.projectAndSourceContext;
                    flow from manageContext.projectAndSourceContext to presentStatus.projectAndSourceContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to assessAndPrepare.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to applySemantics.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to generateInterpretationCandidates.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to reviewSourceEngineering.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to assessProjectFit.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to assessCoverage.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to selectDerivationStrategy.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to deriveModelCandidates.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to reviewModelCandidates.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to deriveModelPlacement.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to reviewModelPlacement.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to assembleInternalModel.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to validateArchitecture.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to generateSysML.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to validateSysML.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to finalModelReview.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext to maintainEvidence.processingRunContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingAttemptContext to assessAndPrepare.processingAttemptContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingAttemptContext to assessProjectFit.processingAttemptContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingAttemptContext to maintainEvidence.processingAttemptContext;
                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingState to presentStatus.processingState;
                    flow from assessAndPrepare.preparedInformation to applySemantics.preparedInformation;
                    flow from applySemantics.canonicalSubjects to generateInterpretationCandidates.canonicalSubjects;
                    flow from applySemantics.governedInformation to generateInterpretationCandidates.governedInformation;
                    flow from assessAndPrepare.assessmentEvidence to reviewSourceEngineering.assessmentEvidence;
                    flow from applySemantics.semanticEvidence to reviewSourceEngineering.semanticEvidence;
                    flow from generateInterpretationCandidates.candidateSet to reviewSourceEngineering.candidateSet;
                    flow from generateInterpretationCandidates.candidateEvidence to reviewSourceEngineering.candidateEvidence;
                    flow from assessAndPrepare.sourceProjection to assessProjectFit.sourceProjection;
                    flow from reviewSourceEngineering.approvedInformation to assessProjectFit.approvedInformation;
                    flow from assessProjectFit.projectFitAssessment to admitProjectContributions.projectFitAssessment;
                    flow from reviewSourceEngineering.approvedInformation to admitProjectContributions.approvedInformation;
                    flow from admitProjectContributions.admittedInformation to assessCoverage.admittedInformation;
                    flow from admitProjectContributions.admittedInformation to selectDerivationStrategy.admittedInformation;
                    flow from admitProjectContributions.admittedInformation to deriveModelCandidates.admittedInformation;
                    flow from assessCoverage.coverageAssessment to deriveModelCandidates.coverageAssessment;
                    flow from selectDerivationStrategy.derivationContext to deriveModelCandidates.derivationContext;
                    flow from deriveModelCandidates.modelCandidateSet to reviewModelCandidates.modelCandidateSet;
                    flow from deriveModelCandidates.modelCandidateSet to deriveModelPlacement.modelCandidateSet;
                    flow from reviewModelCandidates.reviewDecision to deriveModelPlacement.candidateReviewDecision;
                    flow from deriveModelPlacement.placementProposals to reviewModelPlacement.placementProposals;
                    flow from deriveModelCandidates.modelCandidateSet to assembleInternalModel.modelCandidateSet;
                    flow from reviewModelPlacement.approvedPlacementSet to assembleInternalModel.approvedPlacementSet;
                    flow from admitProjectContributions.admittedInformation to assembleInternalModel.admittedInformation;
                    flow from assembleInternalModel.internalEngineeringModel to validateArchitecture.internalEngineeringModel;
                    flow from validateArchitecture.validatedInternalModel to generateSysML.validatedInternalModel;
                    flow from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet to validateSysML.generatedArtifactSet;
                    flow from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet to finalModelReview.generatedArtifactSet;
                    flow from validateSysML.validationResult to finalModelReview.validationResult;
                    flow from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet to publicationGate.generatedArtifactSet;
                    flow from validateSysML.validationResult to publicationGate.validationResult;
                    flow from finalModelReview.finalReviewDecision to publicationGate.finalReviewDecision;
                    flow from assessAndPrepare.sourceProjection to maintainEvidence.sourceProjection;
                    flow from assessAndPrepare.assessmentEvidence to maintainEvidence.assessmentEvidence;
                    flow from applySemantics.semanticEvidence to maintainEvidence.semanticEvidence;
                    flow from generateInterpretationCandidates.candidateEvidence to maintainEvidence.candidateEvidence;
                    flow from reviewSourceEngineering.approvedInformation to maintainEvidence.approvedInformation;
                    flow from assessProjectFit.projectFitAssessment to maintainEvidence.projectFitAssessment;
                    flow from assessCoverage.coverageAssessment to maintainEvidence.coverageAssessment;
                    flow from deriveModelCandidates.modelCandidateSet to maintainEvidence.modelCandidateSet;
                    flow from reviewModelPlacement.approvedPlacementSet to maintainEvidence.approvedPlacementSet;
                    flow from assembleInternalModel.internalEngineeringModel to maintainEvidence.internalEngineeringModel;
                    flow from validateArchitecture.architectureFindings to maintainEvidence.architectureFindings;
                    flow from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet to maintainEvidence.generatedArtifactSet;
                    flow from validateSysML.validationResult to maintainEvidence.validationResult;
                    flow from reviewSourceEngineering.reviewDecision to maintainEvidence.sourceReviewDecision;
                    flow from reviewModelCandidates.reviewDecision to maintainEvidence.modelCandidateReviewDecision;
                    flow from reviewModelPlacement.placementReviewDecision to maintainEvidence.placementReviewDecision;
                    flow from finalModelReview.finalReviewDecision to maintainEvidence.finalReviewDecision;
                    flow from publicationGate.publicationAuthorization to maintainEvidence.publicationAuthorization;
                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingEvidence to reviewSourceEngineering.processingEvidence;
                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingEvidence to assessCoverage.processingEvidence;
                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingEvidence to reviewModelCandidates.processingEvidence;
                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingEvidence to reviewModelPlacement.processingEvidence;
                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingEvidence to finalModelReview.processingEvidence;
                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingEvidence to presentStatus.processingEvidence;
                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingHistory to presentStatus.processingHistory;
                    flow from assessCoverage.coverageAssessment to presentStatus.coverageAssessment;
                    flow from reviewSourceEngineering.reviewDecision to presentStatus.sourceReviewDecision;
                    flow from reviewModelCandidates.reviewDecision to presentStatus.modelCandidateReviewDecision;
                    flow from reviewModelPlacement.placementReviewDecision to presentStatus.placementReviewDecision;
                    flow from finalModelReview.finalReviewDecision to presentStatus.finalReviewDecision;
                    flow from validateSysML.validationResult to presentStatus.validationResult;
                    flow from publicationGate.publicationAuthorization to presentStatus.publicationAuthorization;
                }
            }
            package '02_EngineeringLifecycle' {
                action def <SFB_002> 'Engineering Transformation Lifecycle' {
                    doc
                    /* Control-oriented lifecycle for transformation of source-grounded
                     * engineering information into a validated and Human-authorized
                     * SysML v2 artifact set.
                     *
                     * Source-local processing and Human Engineering Review establish
                     * source-local engineering authority before Project Fit is assessed.
                     *
                     * Only Project-admitted source-local authority inputs may contribute
                     * to Project-level model synthesis.
                     *
                     * Human Model Placement Review precedes deterministic model assembly.
                     * SysML v2 generation and SysML v2 validation are separate lifecycle
                     * stages.
                     *
                     * Final publication requires both successful mandatory validation
                     * and explicit Human approval of the exact generated artifact revision.
                     *
                     * Rework that changes previously approved engineering information
                     * returns to the applicable upstream Human-review boundary.
                     */
                    first start;
                    then action manageContext : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context';
                    then action configureRun : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                    then merge sourceProcessingEntry;
                    then action assessInput : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {
                        out assessmentReady : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide assessmentGate;
                    if assessInput.assessmentReady then semanticEntry;
                    else reviewInput;
                    action reviewInput : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        out acceptInput : Boolean;
                        out requestInputChanges : Boolean;
                        out rejectInput : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide inputReviewGate;
                    if reviewInput.requestInputChanges then sourceProcessingEntry;
                    if reviewInput.acceptInput then semanticEntry;
                    if reviewInput.rejectInput then stopRun;
                    merge semanticEntry;
                    then action applySemanticGovernance : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Apply Semantic Governance' {
                        out semanticReady : Boolean;
                        out semanticResolutionRequired : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide semanticGate;
                    if applySemanticGovernance.semanticReady then sourceCoverageEntry;
                    if applySemanticGovernance.semanticResolutionRequired then generateSemanticCandidates;
                    action generateSemanticCandidates : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates';
                    then action reviewSemanticResolution : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        out semanticResolved : Boolean;
                        out requestSemanticChanges : Boolean;
                        out rejectSemanticResolution : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide semanticReviewGate;
                    if reviewSemanticResolution.semanticResolved then semanticEntry;
                    if reviewSemanticResolution.requestSemanticChanges then generateSemanticCandidates;
                    if reviewSemanticResolution.rejectSemanticResolution then stopRun;
                    merge sourceCoverageEntry;
                    then action assessSourceEvidenceCoverage : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency' {
                        out evidenceSufficient : Boolean;
                        out evidenceDeficiencyRequiresReview : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide sourceCoverageGate;
                    if assessSourceEvidenceCoverage.evidenceSufficient then sourceReviewEntry;
                    if assessSourceEvidenceCoverage.evidenceDeficiencyRequiresReview then reviewEvidenceDeficiency;
                    action reviewEvidenceDeficiency : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        out requestAdditionalInformation : Boolean;
                        out requestAlternativeInterpretation : Boolean;
                        out acceptKnownEvidenceGap : Boolean;
                        out rejectForInsufficientEvidence : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide evidenceReviewGate;
                    if reviewEvidenceDeficiency.requestAdditionalInformation then sourceProcessingEntry;
                    if reviewEvidenceDeficiency.requestAlternativeInterpretation then semanticEntry;
                    if reviewEvidenceDeficiency.acceptKnownEvidenceGap then sourceReviewEntry;
                    if reviewEvidenceDeficiency.rejectForInsufficientEvidence then stopRun;
                    merge sourceReviewEntry;
                    then action reviewSourceEngineering : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        out approveSourceInformation : Boolean;
                        out requestSourceChanges : Boolean;
                        out rejectSourceInformation : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide sourceReviewGate;
                    if reviewSourceEngineering.approveSourceInformation then projectFitEntry;
                    if reviewSourceEngineering.requestSourceChanges then sourceProcessingEntry;
                    if reviewSourceEngineering.rejectSourceInformation then stopRun;
                    merge projectFitEntry;
                    then action assessProjectFit : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {
                        out projectFitAdmitted : Boolean;
                        out projectFitReassessmentRequired : Boolean;
                        out projectFitRejected : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide projectFitGate;
                    if assessProjectFit.projectFitAdmitted then enforceProjectFit;
                    if assessProjectFit.projectFitReassessmentRequired then sourceProcessingEntry;
                    if assessProjectFit.projectFitRejected then completeWithoutProjectContribution;
                    action enforceProjectFit : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                    then projectSynthesisEntry;
                    action completeWithoutProjectContribution : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                    then done;
                    merge projectSynthesisEntry;
                    then action prepareProjectSynthesisInputs : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';
                    then merge targetCoverageEntry;
                    then action assessTargetProjectionCoverage : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency' {
                        out targetProjectionReady : Boolean;
                        out unresolvedProjectionRetained : Boolean;
                        out sourceEvidenceReworkRequired : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide targetCoverageGate;
                    if assessTargetProjectionCoverage.targetProjectionReady then derivationStrategyEntry;
                    if assessTargetProjectionCoverage.unresolvedProjectionRetained then derivationStrategyEntry;
                    if assessTargetProjectionCoverage.sourceEvidenceReworkRequired then sourceProcessingEntry;
                    merge derivationStrategyEntry;
                    then action selectModelDerivationStrategy : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';
                    then modelCandidateEntry;
                    merge modelCandidateEntry;
                    then action deriveModelCandidates : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';
                    then action reviewModelCandidates : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        out approveModelCandidateSet : Boolean;
                        out requestCandidateChanges : Boolean;
                        out rejectModelCandidateSet : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide modelCandidateReviewGate;
                    if reviewModelCandidates.approveModelCandidateSet then modelPlacementEntry;
                    if reviewModelCandidates.requestCandidateChanges then modelCandidateEntry;
                    if reviewModelCandidates.rejectModelCandidateSet then assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy;
                    action assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {
                        out alternativeStrategyAvailable : Boolean;
                        out noAlternativeStrategyAvailable : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide alternativeStrategyGate;
                    if assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy.alternativeStrategyAvailable then selectAlternativeDerivationStrategy;
                    if assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy.noAlternativeStrategyAvailable then stopRun;
                    action selectAlternativeDerivationStrategy : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';
                    then modelCandidateEntry;
                    merge modelPlacementEntry;
                    then action deriveModelPlacement : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';
                    then action reviewModelPlacement : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        out approveModelPlacement : Boolean;
                        out requestPlacementChanges : Boolean;
                        out rejectModelPlacement : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide modelPlacementReviewGate;
                    if reviewModelPlacement.approveModelPlacement then modelAssemblyEntry;
                    if reviewModelPlacement.requestPlacementChanges then modelPlacementEntry;
                    if reviewModelPlacement.rejectModelPlacement then stopRun;
                    merge modelAssemblyEntry;
                    then action assembleInternalEngineeringModel : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';
                    then action validateInternalEngineeringModel : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate and Integrate Architecture Content' {
                        out architectureAccepted : Boolean;
                        out modelReworkRequired : Boolean;
                        out placementReworkRequired : Boolean;
                        out upstreamAuthorityReworkRequired : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide internalModelValidationGate;
                    if validateInternalEngineeringModel.architectureAccepted then artifactGenerationEntry;
                    if validateInternalEngineeringModel.modelReworkRequired then modelCandidateEntry;
                    if validateInternalEngineeringModel.placementReworkRequired then modelPlacementEntry;
                    if validateInternalEngineeringModel.upstreamAuthorityReworkRequired then sourceProcessingEntry;
                    merge artifactGenerationEntry;
                    then action generateSysMLArtifacts : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate SysML v2 Architecture Artifacts' {
                        out generationSuccessful : Boolean;
                        out serializationCorrectionRequired : Boolean;
                        out internalModelChangeRequired : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide generationGate;
                    if generateSysMLArtifacts.generationSuccessful then artifactValidationEntry;
                    if generateSysMLArtifacts.serializationCorrectionRequired then artifactGenerationEntry;
                    if generateSysMLArtifacts.internalModelChangeRequired then modelCandidateEntry;
                    merge artifactValidationEntry;
                    then action validateGeneratedSysML : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' {
                        out validationPassed : Boolean;
                        out serializationCorrectionRequired : Boolean;
                        out internalModelChangeRequired : Boolean;
                        out unrecoverableValidationFailure : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide sysmlValidationGate;
                    if validateGeneratedSysML.validationPassed then finalReviewEntry;
                    if validateGeneratedSysML.serializationCorrectionRequired then artifactGenerationEntry;
                    if validateGeneratedSysML.internalModelChangeRequired then modelCandidateEntry;
                    if validateGeneratedSysML.unrecoverableValidationFailure then stopRun;
