                    merge finalReviewEntry;
                    then action finalModelReview : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        out approvePublication : Boolean;
                        out modelChangeRequired : Boolean;
                        out serializationChangeRequired : Boolean;
                        out rejectPublication : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide finalReviewGate;
                    if finalModelReview.approvePublication then publicationGateEntry;
                    if finalModelReview.modelChangeRequired then modelCandidateEntry;
                    if finalModelReview.serializationChangeRequired then artifactGenerationEntry;
                    if finalModelReview.rejectPublication then stopRun;
                    merge publicationGateEntry;
                    then action enforcePublicationGate : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {
                        out publicationAuthorized : Boolean;
                        out publicationBlocked : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide publicationGate;
                    if enforcePublicationGate.publicationAuthorized then recordPublication;
                    if enforcePublicationGate.publicationBlocked then stopRun;
                    action recordPublication : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                    then action completeRun : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                    then done;
                    action stopRun : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                    then done;
                }
            }
            package '03_ReviewRecoveryAndSupersession' {
                action def <SFB_003> 'Review Recovery and Supersession' {
                    doc
                    /* Operational control behavior for technical retry, review-driven rework,
                     * alternative model derivation, interrupted publication recovery and
                     * material-change supersession.
                     *
                     * A Processing Run may continue through a new Processing Attempt only
                     * when its authoritative run bindings remain unchanged.
                     *
                     * A material binding change requires an explicit successor Processing Run.
                     *
                     * Rejection of a Model Candidate Set may trigger an alternative permitted
                     * derivation strategy before model synthesis is abandoned.
                     *
                     * Publication recovery preserves the exact generated artifact, validation
                     * and Human-release authority boundaries.
                     *
                     * Earlier processing evidence remains retained and traceable.
                     */
                    first start;
                    then action classifyContinuationCondition : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {
                        out normalContinuation : Boolean;
                        out technicalFailure : Boolean;
                        out interruptedPublication : Boolean;
                        out reviewChangesRequested : Boolean;
                        out modelCandidateRejected : Boolean;
                        out otherReviewedTargetRejected : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide continuationConditionGate;
                    if classifyContinuationCondition.normalContinuation then resumeStage;
                    if classifyContinuationCondition.technicalFailure then recordTechnicalFailure;
                    if classifyContinuationCondition.interruptedPublication then assessPublicationRecoveryState;
                    if classifyContinuationCondition.reviewChangesRequested then assessChangeMateriality;
                    if classifyContinuationCondition.modelCandidateRejected then assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy;
                    if classifyContinuationCondition.otherReviewedTargetRejected then recordReviewRejection;
                    action recordTechnicalFailure : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                    then action assessRetryCompatibility : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {
                        out bindingsUnchanged : Boolean;
                        out bindingsChanged : Boolean;
                        out retryNotPermitted : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide retryCompatibilityGate;
                    if assessRetryCompatibility.bindingsUnchanged then createNewAttempt;
                    if assessRetryCompatibility.bindingsChanged then createSuccessorRun;
                    if assessRetryCompatibility.retryNotPermitted then blockRun;
                    action assessChangeMateriality : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {
                        out bindingsUnchanged : Boolean;
                        out bindingsChanged : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide changeMaterialityGate;
                    if assessChangeMateriality.bindingsUnchanged then createNewAttempt;
                    if assessChangeMateriality.bindingsChanged then createSuccessorRun;
                    action assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {
                        out alternativeStrategyAvailable : Boolean;
                        out noAlternativeStrategyAvailable : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide alternativeDerivationGate;
                    if assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy.alternativeStrategyAvailable then selectAlternativeDerivationStrategy;
                    if assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy.noAlternativeStrategyAvailable then recordReviewRejection;
                    action selectAlternativeDerivationStrategy : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';
                    then action recordDerivationStrategyChange : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                    then action assessAlternativeStrategyCompatibility : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {
                        out bindingsUnchanged : Boolean;
                        out bindingsChanged : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide alternativeStrategyCompatibilityGate;
                    if assessAlternativeStrategyCompatibility.bindingsUnchanged then createNewAttempt;
                    if assessAlternativeStrategyCompatibility.bindingsChanged then createSuccessorRun;
                    action createNewAttempt : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                    then action retainAttemptEvidence : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                    then resumeStage;
                    action createSuccessorRun : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                    then action retainPredecessorEvidence : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                    then action appendSupersessionEvent : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                    then resumeStage;
                    action assessPublicationRecoveryState : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability' {
                        out publicationAlreadyComplete : Boolean;
                        out publicationCanResume : Boolean;
                        out publicationHistoryInconsistent : Boolean;
                    }
                    then decide publicationRecoveryGate;
                    if assessPublicationRecoveryState.publicationAlreadyComplete then completePublicationHistory;
                    if assessPublicationRecoveryState.publicationCanResume then resumePublicationGate;
                    if assessPublicationRecoveryState.publicationHistoryInconsistent then blockRun;
                    action completePublicationHistory : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                    then resumeStage;
                    action resumePublicationGate : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                    then done;
                    action recordReviewRejection : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval';
                    then action retainReviewRejectionEvidence : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                    then action completeWithoutPublication : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                    then done;
                    action blockRun : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                    then action presentRecoveryAttention : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Present Processing Status and History';
                    then done;
                    action resumeStage : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                    then done;
                }
            }
            package '04_EngineeringDataFlow' {
                package '00_ProcessingContextDefinitions' {
                    part def 'LLM Inference Service' {
                        doc
                        /* External inference capability used by selected processing steps.
                         * LLM output is non-authoritative processing evidence.
                         * The service has no engineering approval authority.
                         */
                    }
                }
                action def <SFB_005> 'Engineering Information Processing Flow' {
                    doc
                    /* Detailed source-local processing behavior from a registered
                     * Engineering Source to the Human Engineering Review boundary.
                     * Source Projection and Source Analysis Unit identity are established
                     * before source-grounded Evidence Detection.
                     * Canonical Engineering Subjects are established before Persona
                     * Interpretation so that all configured Personas interpret the same
                     * source-grounded engineering subjects.
                     * Persona Interpretation, consensus and variance assessment, controlled
                     * semantic governance and evidence-coverage assessment prepare the
                     * persisted Human Engineering Review package.
                     * Project Fit and Project-level model derivation are outside this
                     * source-local behavior and occur only after explicit Human Engineering
                     * approval.
                     */
                    in item engineeringSource : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringSourceArtifact;
                    in item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                    in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                    out item sourceProjection : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;
                    out item reviewPackage : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ReviewPackage;
                    ref part llmService : '00_ProcessingContextDefinitions'::'LLM Inference Service';
                    ref part humanReviewer : '01_Stakeholder'::'00_Stakeholders'::HumanRole {
                        doc
                        /* External Human engineering authority at the Human Engineering Review
                         * boundary.
                         *
                         * The Human Reviewer evaluates the persisted Review Package. Automated
                         * processing and LLM-supported interpretation do not substitute this
                         * authority.
                         */
                    }
                    action def PersonaCanonicalInterpretation {
                        doc
                        /* One execution represents one configured Persona interpreting
                         * the same persisted Canonical Engineering Subject population
                         * and its source-grounded evidence.
                         *
                         * Repeated executions of the same Persona remain separate
                         * processing observations and do not constitute additional
                         * Persona votes.
                         */
                        in item sourceEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet;
                        in item canonicalSubjects : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        out item interpretation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation;
                    }
                    first start;
                    then P01;
                    action <P01> 'Create Deterministic Source Projection' : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {
                        in item engineeringSource : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringSourceArtifact;
                        in item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                        out item sourceProjection : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;
                    }
                    action <P02> 'Establish Source Analysis Units' : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {
                        in item sourceProjection : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        out item sourceAnalysisUnits : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceAnalysisUnitSet;
                    }
                    action <P03> 'Detect Source-Grounded Evidence' : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {
                        in item sourceAnalysisUnits : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceAnalysisUnitSet;
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        out item sourceEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet;
                    }
                    action <P04> 'Discover Canonical Engineering Subjects' : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Apply Semantic Governance' {
                        in item sourceEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet;
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        out item canonicalSubjects : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;
                    }
                    action <P05> 'Interpret Canonical Subjects with Configured Personas' : PersonaCanonicalInterpretation [1..*];
                    first P01 then P02;
                    first P02 then P03;
                    first P03 then P04;
                    first P04 then P05;
                    action assessPersonaMultiplicity : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates' {
                        in item personaInterpretations : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation [*];
                        out hasMultiplePersonas : Boolean;
                    }
                    first P05 then assessPersonaMultiplicity;
                    then decide personaMultiplicityGate;
                    if assessPersonaMultiplicity.hasMultiplePersonas then P06;
                    else personaAssessmentComplete;
                    action <P06> 'Assess Persona Consensus and Variance' : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates' {
                        in item personaInterpretations : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation [*];
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        out item consensusVariance : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ConsensusVarianceAssessment;
                    }
                    then personaAssessmentComplete;
                    merge personaAssessmentComplete;
                    then P07;
                    action <P07> 'Apply Controlled Semantic Governance' : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Apply Semantic Governance' {
                        in item sourceEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet;
                        in item canonicalSubjects : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;
                        in item personaInterpretations : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation [*];
                        in item consensusVariance : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ConsensusVarianceAssessment [0..1];
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        out item governedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation;
                        out item semanticEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticAssessmentEvidence;
                    }
                    then P08;
                    action <P08> 'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency' : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency' {
                        in item governedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation;
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        out item coverageAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;
                    }
                    then P09;
                    action <P09> 'Prepare Human Review Package' : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        in item canonicalSubjects : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;
                        in item governedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation;
                        in item semanticEvidence : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticAssessmentEvidence;
                        in item consensusVariance : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ConsensusVarianceAssessment [0..1];
                        in item coverageAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        out item reviewPackage : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ReviewPackage;
                    }
                    then done;
                    binding bind P01.engineeringSource = engineeringSource;
                    binding bind P01.projectAndSourceContext = projectAndSourceContext;
                    binding bind P01.sourceProjection = sourceProjection;
                    binding bind P02.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P03.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P04.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P05.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P06.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P07.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P08.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P09.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P09.reviewPackage = reviewPackage;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection from P01.sourceProjection to P02.sourceProjection;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceAnalysisUnitSet from P02.sourceAnalysisUnits to P03.sourceAnalysisUnits;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet from P03.sourceEvidence to P04.sourceEvidence;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet from P03.sourceEvidence to P05.sourceEvidence;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet from P04.canonicalSubjects to P05.canonicalSubjects;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation from P05.interpretation to assessPersonaMultiplicity.personaInterpretations;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation from P05.interpretation to P06.personaInterpretations;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation from P05.interpretation to P07.personaInterpretations;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet from P03.sourceEvidence to P07.sourceEvidence;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet from P04.canonicalSubjects to P07.canonicalSubjects;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet from P04.canonicalSubjects to P09.canonicalSubjects;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ConsensusVarianceAssessment from P06.consensusVariance to P07.consensusVariance;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ConsensusVarianceAssessment from P06.consensusVariance to P09.consensusVariance;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation from P07.governedInformation to P08.governedInformation;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation from P07.governedInformation to P09.governedInformation;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticAssessmentEvidence from P07.semanticEvidence to P09.semanticEvidence;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment from P08.coverageAssessment to P09.coverageAssessment;
                    dependency from P03 to llmService;
                    dependency from P05 to llmService;
                    dependency from P07 to llmService;
                    dependency from P09 to humanReviewer;
                }
                action def <SFB_004> 'Engineering Data Transformation Flow' {
                    doc
                    /* Data-oriented overview of the nominal transformation of registered
                     * Engineering Source information into published SysML v2 artifacts.
                     *
                     * The behavior intentionally remains compact for engineering
                     * communication and presentation.
                     *
                     * Complex authority and model-synthesis stages are represented by
                     * collapsed actions. Their detailed gates, review decisions, rework
                     * and recovery behavior are modeled in SFB_001, SFB_002 and SFB_003.
                     *
                     * The overview therefore shows the path of engineering data through
                     * the system without reproducing the complete control lifecycle.
                     */
                    ref part humanReviewer : '01_Stakeholder'::'00_Stakeholders'::HumanRole;
                    ref part llmService : '00_ProcessingContextDefinitions'::'LLM Inference Service';
                    action def ProjectFitAdmission {
                        doc
                        /* Compact representation of Project Fit assessment and enforcement.
                         *
                         * The behavior admits source-local Approved Engineering Information
                         * only when its exact source projection and processing context satisfy
                         * the applicable Project Fit conditions.
                         */
                        in item sourceProjection : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;
                        in item approvedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;
                        in item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        in item processingAttemptContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;
                        out item admittedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                        action assessFit : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {
                            in item sourceProjection : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;
                            in item approvedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;
                            in item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                            in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                            in item processingAttemptContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;
                            out item projectFitAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectFitAssessment;
                        }
                        action enforceFit : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {
                            in item projectFitAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectFitAssessment;
                            in item approvedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;
                            out item admittedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                        }
                        first assessFit then enforceFit;
                        binding bind assessFit.sourceProjection = sourceProjection;
                        binding bind assessFit.approvedInformation = approvedInformation;
                        binding bind assessFit.projectAndSourceContext = projectAndSourceContext;
                        binding bind assessFit.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                        binding bind assessFit.processingAttemptContext = processingAttemptContext;
                        binding bind enforceFit.approvedInformation = approvedInformation;
                        binding bind enforceFit.admittedInformation = admittedInformation;
                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectFitAssessment from assessFit.projectFitAssessment to enforceFit.projectFitAssessment;
                    }
                    action def ProjectModelSynthesis {
                        doc
                        /* Compact representation of Project-level model synthesis.
                         *
                         * Internally this behavior covers Project-level coverage assessment,
                         * derivation-strategy selection, unified Model Candidate derivation,
                         * Human Candidate Review, Model Placement, Human Model Placement
                         * Review and deterministic Internal Engineering Model assembly.
                         *
                         * These internal stages are intentionally collapsed into one node in
                         * the presentation-oriented end-to-end data-flow view.
                         */
                        in item admittedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                        in item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        out item internalEngineeringModel : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel;
                        action assessCoverage : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency' {
                            in item admittedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                            out item coverageAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;
                        }
                        action selectStrategy : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {
                            in item admittedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                            out item derivationContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelDerivationContext;
                        }
                        action deriveCandidates : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {
                            in item admittedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                            in item coverageAssessment : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;
                            in item derivationContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelDerivationContext;
                            out item modelCandidateSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;
                        }
                        action reviewCandidates : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                            in item modelCandidateSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;
                            out item candidateReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        }
                        action derivePlacement : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {
                            in item modelCandidateSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;
                            in item candidateReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                            out item placementProposals : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelPlacementProposalSet;
                        }
                        action reviewPlacement : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                            in item placementProposals : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelPlacementProposalSet;
                            out item approvedPlacementSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedModelPlacementSet;
                        }
                        action assembleModel : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {
                            in item admittedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                            in item modelCandidateSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;
                            in item approvedPlacementSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedModelPlacementSet;
                            out item internalEngineeringModel : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel;
                        }
                        first assessCoverage then selectStrategy;
                        first selectStrategy then deriveCandidates;
                        first deriveCandidates then reviewCandidates;
                        first reviewCandidates then derivePlacement;
                        first derivePlacement then reviewPlacement;
                        first reviewPlacement then assembleModel;
                        binding bind assessCoverage.admittedInformation = admittedInformation;
                        binding bind selectStrategy.admittedInformation = admittedInformation;
                        binding bind deriveCandidates.admittedInformation = admittedInformation;
                        binding bind assembleModel.admittedInformation = admittedInformation;
                        binding bind assembleModel.internalEngineeringModel = internalEngineeringModel;
                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment from assessCoverage.coverageAssessment to deriveCandidates.coverageAssessment;
                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelDerivationContext from selectStrategy.derivationContext to deriveCandidates.derivationContext;
                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet from deriveCandidates.modelCandidateSet to reviewCandidates.modelCandidateSet;
                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet from deriveCandidates.modelCandidateSet to derivePlacement.modelCandidateSet;
                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet from deriveCandidates.modelCandidateSet to assembleModel.modelCandidateSet;
                        flow
                                of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision
                                from reviewCandidates.candidateReviewDecision
                                to derivePlacement.candidateReviewDecision;

                        flow
                                of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelPlacementProposalSet
                                from derivePlacement.placementProposals
                                to reviewPlacement.placementProposals;

                        flow
                                of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedModelPlacementSet
                                from reviewPlacement.approvedPlacementSet
                                to assembleModel.approvedPlacementSet;
                    }
                    action def PublicationRelease {
                        doc
                        /* Compact representation of the final publication gate and
                         * publication-evidence recording.
                         *
                         * Publication authority depends on the exact generated artifact set,
                         * its validation result and the corresponding Human Final Review
                         * decision.
                         */
                        in item generatedArtifactSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                        in item validationResult : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;
                        in item finalReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        out item publicationAuthorization : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization;
                        action enforcePublicationGate : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {
                            in item generatedArtifactSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                            in item validationResult : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;
                            in item finalReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                            out item publicationAuthorization : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization;
                        }
                        action recordPublication : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability' {
                            in item publicationAuthorization : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization;
                        }
                        first enforcePublicationGate then recordPublication;
                        binding bind enforcePublicationGate.generatedArtifactSet = generatedArtifactSet;
                        binding bind enforcePublicationGate.validationResult = validationResult;
                        binding bind enforcePublicationGate.finalReviewDecision = finalReviewDecision;
                        binding bind enforcePublicationGate.publicationAuthorization = publicationAuthorization;
                        flow
                                of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization
                                from enforcePublicationGate.publicationAuthorization
                                to recordPublication.publicationAuthorization;
                    }
                    first start;
                    then registerSource;
                    action registerSource : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' {
                        in item engineeringSource : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringSourceArtifact;
                        out item registeredEngineeringSource : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringSourceArtifact;
                        out item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                    }
                    action configureRun : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {
                        in item projectAndSourceContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                        out item processingRunContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        out item processingAttemptContext : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;
                    }
                    action processInformation : SFB_005;
                    action reviewInformation : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        in item reviewPackage : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ReviewPackage;
                        out item approvedInformation : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;
                    }
                    action projectFit : ProjectFitAdmission;
                    action deriveArchitecture : ProjectModelSynthesis;
                    action validateArchitecture : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate and Integrate Architecture Content' {
                        in item internalEngineeringModel : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel;
                        out item validatedInternalModel : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ValidatedInternalEngineeringModel;
                    }
                    action generateSysML : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate SysML v2 Architecture Artifacts' {
                        in item validatedInternalModel : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ValidatedInternalEngineeringModel;
                        out item generatedArtifactSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                    }
                    action validateSysML : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' {
                        in item generatedArtifactSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                        out item validationResult : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;
                    }
                    action finalReview : '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval' {
                        in item generatedArtifactSet : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                        in item validationResult : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;
                        out item finalReviewDecision : '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                    }
                    action publishModel : PublicationRelease;
                    then done;
                    binding bind processInformation.llmService = llmService;
                    dependency from reviewInformation to humanReviewer;
                    dependency from finalReview to humanReviewer;
                    dependency from processInformation to llmService;
                    flow
                            of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext
                            from registerSource.projectAndSourceContext
                            to configureRun.projectAndSourceContext;

                    flow
                            of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringSourceArtifact
                            from registerSource.registeredEngineeringSource
                            to processInformation.engineeringSource;

                    flow
                            of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext
                            from registerSource.projectAndSourceContext
                            to processInformation.projectAndSourceContext;

                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext from configureRun.processingRunContext to processInformation.processingRunContext;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ReviewPackage from processInformation.reviewPackage to reviewInformation.reviewPackage;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection from processInformation.sourceProjection to projectFit.sourceProjection;
                    flow
                            of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation
                            from reviewInformation.approvedInformation
                            to projectFit.approvedInformation;

                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext from registerSource.projectAndSourceContext to projectFit.projectAndSourceContext;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext from configureRun.processingRunContext to projectFit.processingRunContext;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext from configureRun.processingAttemptContext to projectFit.processingAttemptContext;
                    flow
                            of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet
                            from projectFit.admittedInformation
                            to deriveArchitecture.admittedInformation;

                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext from configureRun.processingRunContext to deriveArchitecture.processingRunContext;
                    flow
                            of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel
                            from deriveArchitecture.internalEngineeringModel
                            to validateArchitecture.internalEngineeringModel;

                    flow
                            of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ValidatedInternalEngineeringModel
                            from validateArchitecture.validatedInternalModel
                            to generateSysML.validatedInternalModel;

                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet to validateSysML.generatedArtifactSet;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet to finalReview.generatedArtifactSet;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult from validateSysML.validationResult to finalReview.validationResult;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet to publishModel.generatedArtifactSet;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult from validateSysML.validationResult to publishModel.validationResult;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision from finalReview.finalReviewDecision to publishModel.finalReviewDecision;
                }
            }
        }

        package '03_ActionFlowViews' {
            doc
            /* Curated views of the System Functional behavior.
             *
             * The presentation-oriented views intentionally expose only selected
             * behavior elements in order to communicate the engineering-data path
             * through the Turing Generator without reproducing the complete control,
             * authority, rework and recovery lifecycle.
             *
             * Detailed behavior remains modeled in 01_SystemBehavior.
             */
            view AFV_EngineeringTransformationLifecycle_Overview : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::afv {
                doc
                /* Technical overview of the Engineering Transformation Lifecycle.
                 * The view exposes SFB_002 as the authoritative control-oriented lifecycle
                 * for source-local processing, Human review, Project Fit, Project-level
                 * model synthesis, Model Placement, deterministic model assembly,
                 * SysML v2 generation and validation, Final Human Model Review and
                 * publication.
                 * Rework paths and lifecycle decisions are retained because they are part
                 * of the authoritative control behavior.
                 */
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'02_EngineeringLifecycle'::SFB_002;
            }
            view GV_EngineeringDataTransformationFlow : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::gv {
                doc
                /* Presentation-oriented end-to-end engineering-data flow.
                 * The view shows the principal transformation stages from a registered
                 * Engineering Source to publication of the generated SysML v2 model.
                 * Detailed source processing is collapsed into Process Information.
                 * Model Candidate derivation, Human Candidate Review, Model Placement,
                 * Human Model Placement Review and deterministic model assembly are
                 * intentionally collapsed into Derive Architecture.
                 * Detailed lifecycle gates, rework and recovery are intentionally
                 * omitted.
                 */
                expose Actions::Action::start;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::registerSource;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::configureRun;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::processInformation;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::reviewInformation;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::projectFit;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::deriveArchitecture;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::validateArchitecture;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::generateSysML;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::validateSysML;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::finalReview;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::publishModel;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::llmService;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::humanReviewer;
                expose Actions::Action::done;
            }
            view GV_EngineeringInformationProcessingFlow : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::afv {
                doc
                /* Presentation-oriented Action Flow View of source-local Engineering
                 * Information Processing.
                 *
                 * The view shows deterministic Source Projection, Source Analysis Units,
                 * source-grounded Evidence Detection, Canonical Engineering Subject
                 * discovery, Persona Interpretation, consensus and variance assessment,
                 * controlled semantic governance, coverage and evidence assessment and
                 * preparation of the Human Engineering Review package.
                 *
                 * Project Fit and Project-level model synthesis are intentionally outside
                 * this view.
                 *
                 * The enclosing SFB_005 behavior is exposed instead of individually
                 * exposing its nested action usages.
                 */
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_005;
            }
        }

    package '03_Logical' {
        package '00_LogicalDefinitions' {
            part def LogicalComponent;
            part def ExternalArchitectureContext;
            part def <EC_01> 'LLM Inference Context' :> ExternalArchitectureContext {
                doc
                /* External LLM inference capability used by selected logical responsibilities.
                 * It supports engineering processing but has no engineering approval authority.
                 */
            }
            part def <EC_02> 'Semantic Reference Knowledge' :> ExternalArchitectureContext {
                doc
                /* Curated semantic reference context including the Turing Core Vocabulary,
                 * BFO 2020, IOF Core and the derived Reference Concept Index.
                 *
                 * Reference knowledge supports terminology and semantic consistency.
                 * It does not create or approve authoritative engineering information.
                 */
            }
            part def <EC_03> 'Target Model and Framework References' :> ExternalArchitectureContext {
                doc
                /* Versioned framework, target-model and projection references constraining
                 * architecture derivation and target-model structuring.
                 */
            }
            part def <EC_04> 'SysML v2 Modeling References' :> ExternalArchitectureContext {
                doc
                /* Controlled SysML v2 language, syntax, validation and example-model references.
                 *
                 * Includes the SysML v2 specification/release material, validated SYSIDE
                 * syntax evidence and reviewed non-normative example-model references such
                 * as Apollo 11.
                 */
            }
            port def LogicalInteractionPort;
            interface def LogicalInteraction {
                end sourceEnd : LogicalInteractionPort;
                end targetEnd : LogicalInteractionPort;
            }
        }

            part def <LC_04> 'Engineering Information Processing' :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {
                doc
                /* Creates processable source projections, assesses and prepares

                 * synthesis.
                 */
                port sourceContextPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port candidateReviewPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port evidencePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                dependency
                        from 'Engineering Information Processing'
                        to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information';

                dependency from 'Engineering Information Processing' to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Apply Semantic Governance';
            }
            part def <LC_05> 'Candidate and Review Governance' :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {
                doc
                /* Generates and compares engineering interpretations and supports the
                 * Human-authority boundaries for source-local Engineering Review,
                 * Model Candidate Review, Model Placement authorization and Final
                 * Human Model Review.
                 * It presents review evidence, captures Human decisions, binds those
                 * decisions to exact reviewed content and controls promotion across
                 * Human-authorized engineering boundaries.
                 * ,                */
                port userInteractionPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port informationProcessingPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port architecturePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port artifactValidationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port evidencePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port processingEvidencePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;

                dependency
                        from 'Candidate and Review Governance'
                        to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates';

                dependency from 'Candidate and Review Governance' to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval';
            }
            part def <LC_06> 'Coverage Evidence and Traceability Management' :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {
                doc
                /* Assesses engineering-information coverage and evidence sufficiency
                 * and maintains processing evidence, Project Fit evidence, processing
                 * history, Human decisions, source and result provenance, source-local
                 * engineering authority traceability, multi-source subject identity
                 * and artifact-integrity references.
                 */
                port userInteractionPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port informationProcessingPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port reviewGovernancePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;

                port reviewEvidencePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;

                dependency
                        from 'Coverage Evidence and Traceability Management'
                        to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
            }
            part def <LC_07> 'Architecture Synthesis and Validation' :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {
                doc
                /* Derives one Project-level Model Candidate Set from admitted
                 * source-local engineering authority inputs, applies the selected
                 * derivation strategy, derives Model Placement proposals and
                 * deterministically assembles the Internal Engineering Model after
                 * Human-authorized placement.
                 * It subsequently assesses architecture-level consistency,
                 * constraints, interfaces and integration compatibility before
                 * SysML v2 representation generation.
                 */
                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port reviewGovernancePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port artifactGenerationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port evidencePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                dependency
                        from 'Architecture Synthesis and Validation'
                        to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';

                dependency
                        from 'Architecture Synthesis and Validation'
                        to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate and Integrate Architecture Content';
            }
            part def <LC_08> 'SysML v2 Artifact Generation' :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {
                doc
                /* Deterministically generates the traceable Architecture-as-Code
                 * artifact set in the supported SysML v2 textual representation,
                 * organizes the generated artifacts according to the selected target
                 * structure and identifies unsupported representation content.
                 * Generated-artifact validation is a separate logical responsibility.
                 */
                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port architecturePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port validationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port evidencePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                dependency
                        from 'SysML v2 Artifact Generation'
                        to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate SysML v2 Architecture Artifacts';
            }
            part def <LC_09> 'Generated SysML v2 Validation' :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {
                doc
                /* Validates the exact generated SysML v2 artifact set, records
                 * validation findings and publication-blocking status and preserves
                 * the generated artifact set without modification during validation.
                 * This responsibility is logically separated from SysML v2 artifact
                 * generation and provides the validation result required by Final
                 * Human Model Review and publication control.
                 */
                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port artifactGenerationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port reviewGovernancePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port evidencePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                dependency
                        from 'Generated SysML v2 Validation'
                        to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts';
            }
        }

package '03_Subsystem' {
    package '00_SubsystemDefinitions' {
        requirement def SubsystemRequirement {
            attribute rationale;
            attribute source;
            attribute scope;
        }
        part def SubsystemLogicalComponent;
    }
    package SBS_01_UserInteractionAndGuidedWorkflow {
        package '01_Requirements' {
            requirement <SBSR_01_001> 'Provide Engineer Interaction Boundary' : '03_Subsystem'::'00_SubsystemDefinitions'::SubsystemRequirement {
                doc /* The subsystem shall provide the engineer-facing interaction boundary for project and source navigation, processing interaction, Human Review interaction and engineering-result presentation through defined application interfaces. */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /* Separates engineer-facing interaction from the authoritative domain services and persistence mechanisms that perform the underlying engineering operations. */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /* SYSC_014 User Interface through Defined Application Interfaces; SYSC_015 Focused MVP User Interface */
                }
                attribute :>> scope {
                    doc /* mvp */
                }
                dependency
                        from 'Provide Engineer Interaction Boundary'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'10_DesignConstraints'::'05_UserInterface'::'User Interface through Defined Application Interfaces';

                dependency
                        from 'Provide Engineer Interaction Boundary'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'10_DesignConstraints'::'05_UserInterface'::'Focused MVP User Interface';
            }
            requirement <SBSR_01_002> 'Derive Non-Authoritative Guided Workflow Projection' : '03_Subsystem'::'00_SubsystemDefinitions'::SubsystemRequirement {
                doc /* The subsystem shall derive the engineer-facing Guided Workflow projection from persisted authoritative engineering state without establishing independent engineering or workflow authority. */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /* Keeps the Guided Workflow a deterministic presentation of authoritative state rather than an additional workflow state machine. */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /* SYSR_133 Derive Guided Workflow State; SYSC_048 Non-Authoritative User Interface State; SYSC_050 Authoritative State Reconstruction after Write */
                }
                attribute :>> scope {
                    doc /* mvp */
                }
                dependency
                        from 'Derive Non-Authoritative Guided Workflow Projection'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Derive Guided Workflow State';

                dependency
                        from 'Derive Non-Authoritative Guided Workflow Projection'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'10_DesignConstraints'::'05_UserInterface'::'Non-Authoritative User Interface State';

                dependency
                        from 'Derive Non-Authoritative Guided Workflow Projection'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'10_DesignConstraints'::'05_UserInterface'::'Authoritative State Reconstruction after Write';
            }
            requirement <SBSR_01_003> 'Present Current Workflow Status' : '03_Subsystem'::'00_SubsystemDefinitions'::SubsystemRequirement {
                doc /* The subsystem shall present the current processing state and applicable processing stage of the selected engineering context to the user. */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /* Provides an engineer-readable representation of the current workflow position without requiring interpretation of technical processing metadata. */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /* SYSR_082 Present Source Processing State; SYSR_083 Present Current Processing Stage */
                }
                attribute :>> scope {
                    doc /* mvp */
                }
                dependency
                        from 'Present Current Workflow Status'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Present Source Processing State';

                dependency
                        from 'Present Current Workflow Status'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Present Current Processing Stage';
            }
            requirement <SBSR_01_004> 'Present Required Human Attention' : '03_Subsystem'::'00_SubsystemDefinitions'::SubsystemRequirement {
                doc /* The subsystem shall identify engineer-visible processing attention conditions and Human review needs together with the condition requiring Human intervention. */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /* Makes required Human decisions visible without requiring the engineer to infer them from processing state or technical artifacts. */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /* SYSR_084 Indicate Processing Attention Conditions; SYSR_085 Identify Results Awaiting Human Review; SYSR_087 Indicate Review-Triggering Condition */
                }
                attribute :>> scope {
                    doc /* mvp */
                }
                dependency
                        from 'Present Required Human Attention'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Indicate Processing Attention Conditions';

                dependency
                        from 'Present Required Human Attention'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Identify Results Awaiting Human Review';

                dependency
                        from 'Present Required Human Attention'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Indicate Review-Triggering Condition';
            }
            requirement <SBSR_01_005> 'Present Next Engineering Action' : '03_Subsystem'::'00_SubsystemDefinitions'::SubsystemRequirement {
                doc /* The subsystem shall present the next permitted engineering action for the current authoritative workflow state. */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /* Guides the engineer through the workflow without requiring reconstruction of the next action from internal lifecycle metadata. */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /* SYSR_131 Present Next Engineering Action */
                }
                attribute :>> scope {
                    doc /* mvp */
                }
                dependency
                        from 'Present Next Engineering Action'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Present Next Engineering Action';
            }
            requirement <SBSR_01_006> 'Provide Progressive Technical Detail' : '03_Subsystem'::'00_SubsystemDefinitions'::SubsystemRequirement {
                doc /* The subsystem shall provide on-demand access to applicable processing, provenance and traceability detail while keeping the default engineer-facing presentation focused on the current engineering task. */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /* Implements the simple-by-default and explainable-on-demand interaction principle without reducing the underlying evidence or traceability. */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /* SYSR_132 Provide On-Demand Technical Detail; SYSC_015 Focused MVP User Interface; SYSC_049 Progressive Disclosure of Technical Detail */
                }
                attribute :>> scope {
                    doc /* mvp */
                }
                dependency
                        from 'Provide Progressive Technical Detail'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Provide On-Demand Technical Detail';

                dependency
                        from 'Provide Progressive Technical Detail'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'10_DesignConstraints'::'05_UserInterface'::'Focused MVP User Interface';

                dependency
                        from 'Provide Progressive Technical Detail'
                        to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'10_DesignConstraints'::'05_UserInterface'::'Progressive Disclosure of Technical Detail';
            }
            requirement <SBSR_01_007> 'Present Processing History' : '03_Subsystem'::'00_SubsystemDefinitions'::SubsystemRequirement {
                doc /* The subsystem shall provide the retained processing history of the selected engineering source or processing run to the user. */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /* Enables inspection of how the current processing state and supporting evidence were produced. */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /* SYSR_117 Present Processing History */
                }
                attribute :>> scope {
                    doc /* mvp */
                }
                dependency from 'Present Processing History' to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'09_GovernanceSourcesAndHistory'::'Present Processing History';
            }
        }

        package '11_CrossSubsystemArchitecture' {
            part def CrossSubsystemBoundary :> '03_Subsystem'::'00_SubsystemDefinitions'::SubsystemLogicalComponent;

            part <SBSLA_10> 'Turing Generator Cross-Subsystem Logical Architecture' {
                doc /* Refines the accepted System Logical Architecture at subsystem level. The nine boundary usages represent the external interaction boundaries of SBS_01 through SBS_09 and do not introduce additional implementation or engineering responsibilities. Each Cross-SBS interaction preserves the direction and intent of the corresponding System Logical Interaction. */

                part sbs01UserInteraction : CrossSubsystemBoundary {
                    port projectNavigationPort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port orchestrationPort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port reviewPort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port statusPort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                }

                part sbs02ProjectSourceContext : CrossSubsystemBoundary {
                    port userInteractionPort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port orchestrationPort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port sourceProcessingPort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port evidencePort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                }

                part sbs03ProcessingOrchestration : CrossSubsystemBoundary {
                    port userInteractionPort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port projectContextPort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port informationProcessingPort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port reviewGovernancePort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port architecturePort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port artifactGenerationPort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port artifactValidationPort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                    port evidencePort : '02_System'::'03_Logical'::'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                }
